\displaystyle\simeq\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\right)^{4}\frac{1}{\omega_{m}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{l}x)\cos(\omega_{m}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\right)^{4}\frac{1}{\omega_{m}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{l}+\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{l}-\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{l}+\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{l}-\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{l}+\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{l}-\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{l}+\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{l}-\omega_{m}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\omega_{m}}\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\right)^{4}\left(4\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{l}\pm\omega_{m}\right)^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{m}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{l}\pm\omega_{m}\right)^{N}}\right),

whereas, for large values of
i,j,l,m
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{l}\pm\omega_{m}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|B_{ijl}^{(m)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{l}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{m}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)\tan(x)e_{j}(x)\tan(x)e_{l}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{m}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{l}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\int_{\cdot}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{m}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\frac{\omega_{l}}{\omega_{m}},

which completes the proof.
∎

4.
Perturbations 2: Finite sum with an error term

Since the series (
2.5
)-(
2.7
) may not converge, we can still assume that
(\Phi,\Pi,A,\delta)
are all close to the AdS solution
(0,0,1,0)
and expand
(\Phi,\Pi,A,\delta)
using (
2.8
)-(
2.10
). First, we solve at the linear level and obtain the periodic expressions

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\Phi_{1}(\tau,x):=\cos(\tau)e^{\prime}_{\gamma}(x),\\
\Pi_{1}(\tau,x):=-\omega_{\gamma}\sin(\tau)e_{\gamma}(x),\\
A_{2}(\tau,x):=\cos^{2}(\tau)\Gamma_{1}(x)+\sin^{2}(\tau)\Gamma_{2}(x),\\
\delta_{2}(\tau,x):=-\cos^{2}(\tau)\Gamma_{3}(x)-\sin^{2}(\tau)\Gamma_{4}(x),\end{cases}

where

\displaystyle\Gamma_{1}(x):=\frac{\cos^{3}(x)}{\sin(x)}\int_{0}^{x}\left(e^{\prime}_{0}(y)\right)^{2}\tan^{2}(y)dy,\quad\Gamma_{2}(x):=\omega_{0}^{2}\frac{\cos^{3}(x)}{\sin(x)}\int_{0}^{x}\left(e_{0}(y)\right)^{2}\tan^{2}(y)dy,

\displaystyle\Gamma_{3}(x):=\int_{0}^{x}\left(e^{\prime}_{0}(y)\right)^{2}\sin(y)\cos(y)dy,\quad\Gamma_{4}(x):=\omega_{0}^{2}\int_{0}^{x}\left(e_{0}(y)\right)^{2}\sin(y)\cos(y)dy.

For simplicity, we have fixed
\gamma=0
and use Lemma
2.2
we compute

\displaystyle\Gamma_{1}(x)=\frac{3\cos^{2}(x)}{2\pi\tan(x)}\Big(12x-3\sin(2x)-3\sin(4x)+\sin(6x)\Big),

\displaystyle\Gamma_{2}(x)=-\frac{3\cos^{2}(x)}{2\pi\tan(x)}\Big(-12x-3\sin(2x)+3\sin(4x)+\sin(6x)\Big),

\displaystyle\Gamma_{3}(x)=\frac{3\sin^{4}(x)}{2\pi}\Big(25+20\cos(x)+3\cos(4x)\Big),

\displaystyle\Gamma_{4}(x)=-\frac{9}{32\pi}\Big(-93+56\cos(2x)+28\cos(4x)+8\cos(6x)+\cos(8x)\Big).

For future reference, observe that

\displaystyle\left\|\Gamma_{a}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim 1,\quad\forall a\in\{1,2,3,4\}.

(4.1)

Second, we get the following non-linear system for the error terms
(\Psi,\Sigma,B,\Theta)
,

\displaystyle(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\partial_{\tau}\Psi

\displaystyle=(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\eta_{0})\sin(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}(x)+\partial_{x}\Bigg(\Sigma+\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}(x)(A_{2}+\delta_{2})\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}\Bigg(-\Sigma(A_{2}+\delta_{2})-\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}(x)(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\partial_{x}\Bigg(\Sigma(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})-\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}(-\Theta A_{2}+B\delta_{2})\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\partial_{x}\Bigg(\Sigma(-\Theta A_{2}+B\delta_{2})+\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}B\Theta\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{8}\partial_{x}\Big(\Sigma\Theta B\Big),

\displaystyle(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}

\displaystyle\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\partial_{\tau}\Sigma=\omega_{0}(\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\eta_{0})\cos(\tau)e_{0}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\frac{4}{\sin(2x)}\left(\Psi-\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}(A_{2}+\delta_{2})\right)-\cos(\tau)\left(e_{0}^{\prime}(A_{2}+\delta_{2})\right)^{\prime}+\partial_{x}\Psi\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(\frac{4}{\sin(2x)}\left(-\Psi(A_{2}+\delta_{2})+\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})\right)-\left(\Psi(A_{2}+\delta_{2})\right)^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\cos(\tau)\left(e_{0}^{\prime}(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})\right)^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(\frac{4}{\sin(2x)}\left(\Psi(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})+\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}(-A_{2}\Theta+\delta_{2}B)\right)

\displaystyle+\cos(\tau)\left(e_{0}^{\prime}(\delta_{2}B-A_{2}\Theta)\right)^{\prime}+\left(\Psi(-B+\Theta+A_{2}\delta_{2})\right)^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\Bigg(\frac{4}{\sin(2x)}\left(\Psi(-\Theta A_{2}+\delta_{2}B)-\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}B\Theta\right)-\cos(\tau)\left(e_{0}^{\prime}B\Theta\right)^{\prime}+\left(\delta_{2}\Psi B\right)^{\prime}-\left(A_{2}\Psi\Theta\right)^{\prime}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{8}\Bigg(\frac{4}{\sin(2x)}B\Theta\Psi+\left(B\Theta\Psi\right)^{\prime}\Bigg),

\displaystyle\partial_{x}B

\displaystyle+\frac{1+2\sin^{2}(x)}{\sin(x)\cos(x)}B=\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\Bigg(2\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}\Psi-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}\Sigma-A_{2}\cos^{2}(\tau)(e_{0}^{\prime})^{2}-\omega_{0}^{2}A_{2}\sin^{2}(\tau)e_{0}^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(\Psi^{2}+\Sigma^{2}-2A_{2}\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}\Psi+2\omega_{0}A_{2}\sin(\tau)e_{0}\Sigma-\cos^{2}(\tau)(e_{0}^{\prime})^{2}B-\omega_{0}^{2}\sin^{2}(\tau)(e_{0})^{2}B\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(-A_{2}\Psi^{2}-A_{2}\Sigma^{2}-2\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}B\Psi+2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}B\Sigma\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{6}\Big(B\left(\Psi^{2}+\Sigma^{2}\right)\Big),

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\Theta

\displaystyle=\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\Bigg(2\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}\Psi-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}\Sigma-\delta_{2}\cos^{2}(\tau)(e_{0}^{\prime})^{2}-\omega_{0}^{2}\delta_{2}\sin^{2}(\tau)e_{0}^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(\Psi^{2}+\Sigma^{2}-2\delta_{2}\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}\Psi+2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}\delta_{2}\Sigma+\cos^{2}(\tau)(e_{0}^{\prime})^{2}\Theta+\omega_{0}^{2}\sin^{2}(\tau)e_{0}^{2}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(-\delta_{2}\Psi^{2}-\delta_{2}\Sigma^{2}+2\cos(\tau)e_{0}^{\prime}\Theta\Psi-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)e_{0}\Theta\Sigma\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{2}\epsilon^{6}\Big(\Theta\left(\Psi^{2}+\Sigma^{2}\right)\Big).

4.1.
Definition of the Fourier constants

As before, we expand the error terms
(\Psi,\Sigma,B,\Theta)
in terms of the eigenfunctions of the linearized operator as follows

\displaystyle\Psi(\tau,x)=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\psi_{i}(\tau)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}},\quad\Sigma(\tau,x)=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\sigma_{i}(\tau)e_{i}(x),

\displaystyle B(\tau,x)=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}b_{i}(\tau)e_{i}(x),\quad\Theta(\tau,x)=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\xi_{i}(\tau)e_{i}(x).

After substituting these expressions into the equations above, we take the inner product
(\cdot|e_{j}^{\prime})
(for the equation for
\Psi
) and
(\cdot|e_{j})
(for the equations for
\Sigma,B
and
\Theta
) in both sides. A long but straightforward computation yields that all the interactions with respect the space variable
x
are included in the following Fourier constants:

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{abij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{a}(x)-\Gamma_{b}(x)\right)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{D}_{abij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)\Gamma_{b}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{1423ij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)\Gamma_{4}(x)-\Gamma_{2}(x)\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{ajki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}_{ijkl}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

for the equation for
\Psi
,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{abij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{a}(x)-\Gamma_{b}(x)\right)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{abij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)\Gamma_{b}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{1423ij}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)\Gamma_{4}(x)-\Gamma_{2}(x)\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{kji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{akji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{kjli}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{l}(x)e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\tan^{2}(x)dx,

for the equation for
\Sigma
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{J}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{I}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{ajki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{Q}_{ajki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{R}_{klji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{jkli}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx,

for the equation for
\Theta
, and finally

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{J}}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{ajki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{ajki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{a}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx,

for the equation for
B
. In addition, the non-linear system for the error terms boils down to

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\psi_{i}(\tau)-\frac{\omega_{i}}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\sigma_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{F}_{i}(\tau)}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}++\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))}{\omega_{i}},

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\sigma_{i}(\tau)+\frac{\omega_{i}}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\psi_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{F}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau)),

subject to the constraints

\displaystyle b_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{b}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\prescript{b}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau)),

\displaystyle\xi_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\xi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau)).

Here, the source terms are given explicitly in terms of the Fourier constants by

\displaystyle\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{F}_{i}(\tau)}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle=\omega_{i}\Big(\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}\theta_{0}\delta_{0,i}}{\omega_{i}^{2}}\sin(\tau)+\omega_{0}\mathbb{C}_{130i}\sin(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)+\omega_{0}\mathbb{C}_{240i}\sin^{3}(\tau)\Big)

\displaystyle+\omega_{i}\epsilon^{2}\Big(\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}\eta_{0}\delta_{0,i}}{\omega_{i}^{2}}\sin(\tau)+\omega_{0}\mathbb{D}_{130i}\sin(\tau)\cos^{4}(\tau)+\omega_{0}\mathbb{D}_{240i}\sin^{5}(\tau)+\omega_{0}\mathbb{E}_{14230i}\sin^{3}(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{F}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\Big(\frac{\theta_{0}\delta_{0,i}}{\omega_{i}}\cos(\tau)+\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{130i}\cos^{3}(\tau)+\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{240i}\cos(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\Big)

\displaystyle+\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\epsilon^{2}\Big(\frac{\eta_{0}\delta_{0,i}}{\omega_{i}}\cos(\tau)+\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{130i}\cos^{5}(\tau)+\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{240i}\cos^{4}(\tau)\sin^{4}(\tau)+\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{14230i}\cos^{3}(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\omega_{0}^{2}\Big(-\cos^{4}(\tau)\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{100i}-\sin^{2}(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{200i}-\sin^{2}(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{100i}-\sin^{4}(\tau)\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{200i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{\omega_{i}}\Big(\cos^{4}(\tau)\mathbb{P}_{300i}+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\mathbb{P}_{400i}+\sin^{4}(\tau)\mathbb{Q}_{400i}+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\mathbb{Q}_{300i}\Big),

where
\delta_{0,i}
stands for the Kronecker’s delta, whereas the linear and non-linear terms are given by

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))=\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{C}_{13ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{C}_{24ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle-\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{G}_{0ji}(\xi_{j}(\tau)-b_{j}(\tau))\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(-\cos^{4}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{D}_{13ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{E}_{1423ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)+\sin^{4}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{D}_{24ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{10ji}\xi_{j}(\tau)+\omega_{0}\sin^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{20ji}\xi_{j}(\tau)+\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{30ji}b_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\omega_{0}\sin^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{40ji}b_{j}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{G}_{jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)(-b_{k}(\tau)+\xi_{k}(\tau))\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\Bigg(\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{H}_{0jki}b_{j}(\tau)\xi_{k}(\tau)-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{2jki}\xi_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{4jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)b_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{1jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\xi_{k}(\tau)-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{F}_{3jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)b_{k}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{8}\Bigg(-\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}^{2}\mathbb{H}_{jkli}\xi_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)b_{l}(\tau)\Bigg),

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))=\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{13ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)+\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{24ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle-\cos(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{0ji}(\xi_{j}(\tau)-b_{j}(\tau))\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(\cos^{4}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{13ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{1423ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)+\sin^{4}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{24ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\sin^{2}(\tau)\cos(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{20ji}\xi_{j}(\tau)+\cos^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{10ji}\xi_{j}(\tau)+\cos^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{30ji}b_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\cos(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{40ji}b_{j}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)(b_{k}(\tau)-\xi_{k}(\tau))\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\Bigg(\cos(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{0jki}b_{j}(\tau)\xi_{k}(\tau)+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{1jki}\xi_{k}(\tau)\psi_{j}(\tau)+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{3jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)b_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{2jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)\xi_{k}(\tau)+\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{F}}_{4jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)b_{k}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{8}\Bigg(\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{ljki}\xi_{k}(\tau)\psi_{l}(\tau)b_{j}(\tau)\Bigg),

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))=\Bigg(2\cos(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{J}}_{0ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{0ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(-2\cos^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{10ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)-2\cos(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{20ji}\psi_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+2\omega_{0}\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{10ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)+2\omega_{0}\sin^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{20ji}\sigma_{j}(\tau)-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}^{2}\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{00ji}b_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}^{2}\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{00ji}b_{j}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{J}}_{jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{1jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{2jki}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle-2\cos(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{0kji}\psi_{k}(\tau)b_{j}(\tau)-\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{1jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle-\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{2jki}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)+2\sin(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{0jki}\sigma_{k}(\tau)b_{j}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\Bigg(-\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji}\psi_{k}(\tau)\psi_{l}(\tau)b_{j}(\tau)-\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}\sigma_{k}(\tau)\sigma_{l}(\tau)b_{j}(\tau)\Bigg),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))=\Bigg(2\cos(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\frac{\mathbb{J}_{0ji}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{I}_{0ji}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(2\cos^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{30ji}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)+2\cos(\tau)\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{40ji}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)-2\omega_{0}\sin^{3}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{40ji}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle-2\omega_{0}\cos^{2}(\tau)\sin(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{30ji}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}^{2}\frac{\mathbb{R}_{00ji}}{\omega_{i}}\xi_{j}(\tau)+\omega_{0}^{2}\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\omega_{i}\frac{\mathbb{S}_{00ji}}{\omega_{i}^{2}}\xi_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{J}_{jki}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{I}_{jki}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{4}\Bigg(\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{3jki}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)+\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{P}_{4jki}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{j}(\tau)\psi_{k}(\tau)+2\cos(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\omega_{0}\frac{\mathbb{R}_{0kji}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{k}(\tau)\xi_{j}(\tau)

\displaystyle+\cos^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{3jki}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)+\sin^{2}(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{4jki}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{j}(\tau)\sigma_{k}(\tau)-2\omega_{0}\sin(\tau)\sum_{j,k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{S}_{0jki}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{k}(\tau)\xi{j}(\tau)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{6}\Bigg(-\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{R}_{klji}}{\omega_{i}}\psi_{k}(\tau)\psi_{l}(\tau)\xi_{j}(\tau)+\sum_{j,k,l=0}^{\infty}\frac{\mathbb{S}_{jkli}}{\omega_{i}}\sigma_{k}(\tau)\sigma_{l}(\tau)\xi_{j}(\tau)\Bigg).

4.2.
Approximate periodic solution and small divisors

Similarly to the first approach with the infinite sum, the linear and homogeneous part of the ordinary differential equation for
(\psi_{i},\sigma_{i})
is simply the equation for the harmonic oscillator and hence we can use the variational constants formula to solve it. We find the fixed-point formulation

\displaystyle\psi_{i}(\tau)=\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\frac{\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(s),\sigma(s),b(s),\xi(s))}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(s),\sigma(s),b(s),\xi(s))\Bigg)ds,

\displaystyle\sigma_{i}(\tau)=\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\frac{-\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(s),\sigma(s),b(s),\xi(s))}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(s),\sigma(s),b(s),\xi(s))\Bigg)ds,

subject to the constrain equations

\displaystyle b_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{b}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\prescript{b}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau)),

\displaystyle\xi_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\xi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)+\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathfrak{N}}_{i}(\psi(\tau),\sigma(\tau),b(\tau),\xi(\tau))

where

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)\psi_{i}(0)+\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)\sigma_{i}(0)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg(\frac{\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{F}_{i}(s)}{\omega_{i}}+\frac{\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{F}_{i}(s)\Bigg)ds,

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=-\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)\psi_{i}(0)+\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)\sigma_{i}(0)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\Bigg(\frac{-\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\frac{\prescript{\psi}{}{F}_{i}(s)}{\omega_{i}}+\frac{\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}(\tau-s)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\prescript{\sigma}{}{F}_{i}(s)\Bigg)ds.

Furthermore, one can use various trigonometric identities to write

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{F}_{i}(s)

\displaystyle=K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(s)+\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(3s)+M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(5s),

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{F}_{i}(s)

\displaystyle=N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(s)+\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(3s)+T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(5s)

where

\displaystyle K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}^{2}\Bigg(\frac{\delta_{i,0}\omega_{0}\theta_{0}}{\omega_{i}^{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{C}_{130i}+\frac{3}{4}\mathbb{C}_{240i}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{\delta_{i,0}\omega_{0}\eta_{0}}{\omega_{i}^{2}}+\frac{1}{8}\mathbb{D}_{130i}+\frac{5}{8}\mathbb{D}_{240i}+\frac{1}{8}\mathbb{E}_{14230i}\right)\Bigg),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}^{2}\Bigg(\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{C}_{130i}-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{C}_{240i}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{3}{16}\mathbb{D}_{130i}-\frac{5}{16}\mathbb{D}_{240i}+\frac{1}{16}\mathbb{E}_{14230i}\right)\Bigg),

\displaystyle M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}^{2}\epsilon^{2}\Bigg(\frac{1}{16}\mathbb{D}_{130i}+\frac{1}{16}\mathbb{D}_{240i}-\frac{1}{16}\mathbb{E}_{14230i}\Bigg),

\displaystyle N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\left(\frac{\delta_{i,0}\theta_{0}}{\omega_{i}}+\frac{3}{4}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{130i}+\frac{1}{4}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{240i}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{\delta_{i,0}\eta_{0}}{\omega_{i}}+\frac{5}{32}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{130i}+\frac{63}{64}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{240i}+\frac{19}{32}\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{14230i}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\left(\frac{1}{4}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{130i}-\frac{1}{4}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{240i}+\epsilon^{2}\left(\frac{5}{64}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{130i}+\frac{27}{64}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{240i}+\frac{11}{64}\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{14230i}\right)\right),

\displaystyle T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle:=\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\epsilon^{2}\Big(\frac{1}{16}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{130i}+\frac{5}{64}\overline{\mathbb{D}}_{240i}-\frac{1}{64}\overline{\mathbb{E}}_{14230i}\Big).

Now, computing the integrals above we obtain

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(\tau)+\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(3\tau)+\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(5\tau)

\displaystyle+\Big(\psi_{i}(0)+\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\Big)\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)+\sigma_{i}(0)\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right),

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(\tau)+\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(3\tau)+\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(5\tau)

\displaystyle+\sigma_{i}(0)\cos\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right)-\Big(\psi_{i}(0)-\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\Big)\sin\left(\frac{\omega_{i}\tau}{\omega_{0}+\theta_{0}\epsilon^{2}+\eta_{0}\epsilon^{4}}\right),

where

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-\omega_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{3(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-3\omega_{0}-3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+3\omega_{0}+3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{5(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-5\omega_{0}-5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+5\omega_{0}+5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-\omega_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{3(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-3\omega_{0}-3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+3\omega_{0}+3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{5(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-5\omega_{0}-5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+5\omega_{0}+5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}

and

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-\omega_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{3(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-3\omega_{0}-3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+3\omega_{0}+3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=-\frac{5(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-5\omega_{0}-5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+5\omega_{0}+5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})},

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})K_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}N_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-\omega_{0}-\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{3(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})\Lambda_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}\Xi_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-3\omega_{0}-3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+3\omega_{0}+3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\frac{5(\omega_{0}+\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})M_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\omega_{i}^{2}T_{i}(\epsilon^{2})}{(\omega_{i}-5\omega_{0}-5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}-5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})(\omega_{i}+5\omega_{0}+5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}+5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0})}.

Notice that conditions like

\displaystyle\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{0}\pm\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}\pm\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0}\neq 0,\quad\omega_{i}\pm 3\omega_{0}\pm 3\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}\pm 3\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0}\neq 0,\quad\omega_{i}\pm 5\omega_{0}\pm 5\epsilon^{2}\theta_{0}\pm 5\epsilon^{4}\eta_{0}\neq 0,

are closely related to small divisors and play an important role in KAM theory
[
7
,
4
,
8
,
3
,
5
,
6
]
. Observe that the identity

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})+\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})=0

holds true for all
i=0,1,\dots
and all
\epsilon>0
. Here, all the constants involved

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

depend explicitly on the Fourier constants defined above and most importantly depend only on one index. Due to this fact, we can compute them (see Lemma
B.1
in Appendix B). The fact that these constants are given in closed forms has a numerous advantages. First, we get the asymptotic behaviour for large
i
and fixed
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{5}},\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{5}},\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{5}},\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{5}},\text{~for~}i\longrightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{5}},\text{~for~}i\longrightarrow\infty.

Second, we get their asymptotic behaviour for sufficiently small
\epsilon
and fixed
i=0,1,\dots
,

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\left(-3+\frac{459}{4\pi\theta_{0}}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=0\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 0\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\frac{\omega_{3}}{72\theta_{0}}\left(\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{1303}-\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{2403}+3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}-3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=3\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 3\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=6\\[10.0pt]
(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 6\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\left(-3+\frac{459}{4\pi\theta_{0}}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=0\\[10.0pt]
\frac{\omega_{3}}{72\theta_{0}}\left(\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{1303}-\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{2403}+3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}-3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=3\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=6\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 0,3,6\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\left(-3+\frac{459}{4\pi\theta_{0}}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=0\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 0\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\frac{\omega_{3}}{72\theta_{0}}\left(\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{1303}-\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{2403}+3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}-3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=3\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 3\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=6\\[10.0pt]
(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 6\end{array}\right.,

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\left(-3+\frac{459}{4\pi\theta_{0}}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=0\\[10.0pt]
\frac{\omega_{3}}{72\theta_{0}}\left(\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{1303}-\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{2403}+3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}-3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}\right)\epsilon^{-2}+(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=3\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i=6\\[10.0pt]
(...)1+(...)\epsilon^{2},&\text{for }i\neq 0,3,6\end{array}\right.

We compute

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{1303}=-\frac{291}{560\sqrt{10}\pi},~\mathbb{C}_{2403}=-\frac{99}{140\sqrt{10}\pi},~\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}=-\frac{111}{140\sqrt{10}\pi},~\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}=-\frac{339}{560\sqrt{10}\pi}

and hence

\displaystyle\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{1303}-\omega_{3}\mathbb{C}_{2403}+3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{1303}-3\omega_{0}\overline{\mathbb{C}}_{2403}=0.

Consequently, by the structure of the equations,
\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{3},\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{3},\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{3}
and
\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{3}
cannot blowup as
\epsilon
does to zero. However, we choose

\displaystyle\theta_{0}:=\frac{153}{4\pi}

to ensure that every component of the periodic parts
\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)
and
\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)
of
\psi_{i}
and
\sigma_{i}
respectively are bounded as
\epsilon
goes to zero. This choice coincides with the choice of
\theta_{2}
from the first approach as well as with the numerical computations of Rostworowski-Maliborski
[
30
]
.
Similarly, using various trigonometric identities, we get

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}+\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}\cos(2\tau)+\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}\cos(4\tau),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}+\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}\cos(2\tau)+\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}\cos(4\tau),

where

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{8}\Big(3\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{100i}+\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{200i}+\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{100i}+3\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{200i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{2}\Big(-\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{100i}+\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{200i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{8}\Big(-\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{100i}+\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{200i}+\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{100i}-\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{200i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{8\omega_{i}^{2}}\Big(3\mathbb{P}_{300i}+\mathbb{P}_{400i}+\mathbb{Q}_{300i}+3\mathbb{Q}_{400i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{2\omega_{i}^{2}}\Big(\mathbb{P}_{300i}-\mathbb{Q}_{400i}\Big),

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}}{8\omega_{i}^{2}}\Big(\mathbb{P}_{300i}-\mathbb{P}_{400i}-\mathbb{Q}_{300i}+\mathbb{Q}_{400i}\Big).

As before, all the constants involved

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i},\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i},\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i},\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i},\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i},\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}

depend explicitly on the Fourier constants defined above and most importantly depend only on one index. Due to this fact, we can compute them (see Lemma
B.1
in Appendix B). We immediately get their asymptotic behaviour for large
i
,

\displaystyle\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{12}},\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{12}},\prescript{b}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{12}},\text{~for~}i\longrightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\prescript{\xi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}^{6}},\text{~for~}i\longrightarrow\infty.

4.3.
Choice of the initial data

We choose

\displaystyle\psi_{i}(0):=-\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})=\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{N}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2}),\quad\sigma_{i}(0):=0

so that

\displaystyle\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(\tau)+\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(3\tau)+\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\cos(5\tau),

\displaystyle\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(\tau)+\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(3\tau)+\prescript{\sigma}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\sin(5\tau).

This choice is motivated by the fact that the source terms
\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)
and
\prescript{\sigma}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)
of the solutions
\psi_{i}(\tau)
and
\sigma_{i}(\tau)
would give rise to a periodic term. Indeed,

\displaystyle\Psi(\tau,x)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\psi_{i}(\tau)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\prescript{\psi}{}{S}_{i}(\tau)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\Bigg(\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\Bigg)\cos(\tau)+\Bigg(\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{R}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\Bigg)\cos(3\tau)

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\prescript{\psi}{}{\mathcal{M}}_{i}(\epsilon^{2})\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\Bigg)\cos(5\tau)+\mathcal{O}\left(\epsilon^{2}\right)

and similarly for
\Sigma,B
and
\Theta
.

4.4.
Growth and decay of the Fourier constants

We are interested in the asymptotic behaviour of all the Fourier constants which appear using this approach. To begin with, we split them into five groups as follows

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{\omega_{i}\mathbb{C}_{13ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{C}_{24ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{D}_{13ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{D}_{24ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{E}_{1423ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{C}}_{13ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{C}}_{24ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{D}}_{13ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{D}}_{24ji},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{E}}_{1423ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{10ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{20ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{30ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{40ji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{1jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{2jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{3jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{4jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{G}_{0ji},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{P}_{10ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{P}_{20ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{P}_{30ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{P}_{40ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{J}_{0ji}}{\omega_{i}},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{P}}_{10ji},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{P}}_{20ji},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{P}}_{30ji},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{P}}_{40ji},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{J}}_{0ji}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{1jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{2jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{3jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{4jki},\frac{\omega_{0}\mathbb{R}_{0kji}}{\omega_{i}},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{1jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{2jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{3jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{4jki},\omega_{0}\mathbb{\overline{R}}_{0kji}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{10ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{20ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{30ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{40ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{I}_{0ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\omega_{0}^{2}\mathbb{R}_{00ji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{S}_{00ji}}{\omega_{i}},\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{10ji},\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{20ji},\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{30ji},\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{F}}_{40ji},

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{G}}_{0ji},\mathbb{\overline{Q}}_{10ji},\mathbb{\overline{Q}}_{20ji},\mathbb{\overline{Q}}_{30ji},\mathbb{\overline{Q}}_{40ji},\mathbb{\overline{I}}_{0ji},\omega_{0}^{2}\mathbb{\overline{R}}_{00ji},\mathbb{\overline{S}}_{00ji}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{4}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{\frac{\mathbb{I}_{jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{J}_{jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{P}_{1jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{P}_{2jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{P}_{3jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{P}_{4jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{1jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{2jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{3jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{Q}_{4jki}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{S}_{0jki}}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki},\overline{\mathbb{J}}_{jki},\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{1jki},\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{2jki},\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{3jki},\overline{\mathbb{P}}_{4jki},\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{1jki},\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{2jki},\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{3jki},\overline{\mathbb{Q}}_{4jki},\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{0jki},\omega_{0}\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{0jki}\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}

\displaystyle:=\Bigg\{\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{0jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{G}_{jki},\frac{\mathbb{R}_{klji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{S}_{jkli}}{\omega_{i}},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{G}}_{jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{klji},\omega_{i}\mathbb{\overline{H}}_{ikjl},\mathbb{\overline{R}}_{klji},\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}\Bigg\}.

As before, we shall use the notation

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}f(a\pm b\pm c)=f(a+b+c)+f(a+b-c)+f(a-b+c)+f(a-b-c),

that is summation with respect to all possible combinations of plus and minus and expressions like
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{m}
stand not only for
\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{m}
and
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{m}
but also for
\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{m}
and
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{m}
, that is considering all possible combinations of plus and minus. We will use the leading order terms (Remark
2.3
) together with the asymptotic behavior of the oscillatory integrals (Lemma
2.6
), the orthogonality properties (Lemma
2.2
), the
L^{\infty}-
bounds for quantities related to the eigenfunctions (Lemma
2.4
) and the
L^{\infty}-
bounds of the weights
\Gamma_{a}
(estimate (
4.1
)).

Proof.

All these estimates follow directly from Lemma
2.9
and in particular from

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}F(x)\cos(2bx)dx=\sum_{k=0}^{N}\frac{(-1)^{k}}{a^{2k+2}}\left((-1)^{b}F^{(2k+1)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)-F^{(2k+1)}\left(0\right)\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2N+2}}\right),

as
b\longrightarrow\infty
. However, we illustrate the proof only for the first constant, namely
\omega_{i}\mathbb{C}_{13ji}
. For large values of
i,j
and in the case where both
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\longrightarrow\infty
(equivalently when
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\longrightarrow\infty
),

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{13ji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)\cos((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j})x)dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)\cos((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j})x)dx.

Observe that both
\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}
and
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}
are even,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}=2(3+i+j),\quad\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}=2(i-j).

We define
F(x):=\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)
. If
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\longrightarrow\infty
, then Lemma
2.9
applies and since

\displaystyle F^{\prime}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=F^{\prime}\left(0\right)=0,~~~F^{\prime\prime\prime}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=-54\neq 0

we infer

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{13ji}=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j})^{4}}+\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j})^{4}}\right),

as
i,j\longrightarrow\infty
. On the other hand, if
\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, then we see that

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{C}_{13ji}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Gamma_{1}-\Gamma_{3}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim 1.

In conclusion,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}\mathbb{C}_{13ji}=\Bigg\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j})^{4}}\right),&\text{if }\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\longrightarrow\infty\\
\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}\right),&\text{if }\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty,\end{array}

as
i,j\longrightarrow\infty
.
∎

Proof.

All these estimates follow directly from Lemma
2.9
and in particular from

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}F(x)\sin((2b+1)x)dx=\sum_{k=0}^{N}\frac{(-1)^{k}}{a^{2k+1}}F^{(2k)}\left(0\right)+(-1)^{b}\sum_{k=0}^{N}\frac{(-1)^{k}}{a^{2k+2}}F^{(2k+1)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2N+2}}\right),

as
b\longrightarrow\infty
. However, we illustrate the proof only for the first constant, namely
\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{1jki}
. For large values of
i,j,k
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{1jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{1}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx.

Observe that all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}
are odd,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i-j+k+1)+1,\quad\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i-j-k-2)+1,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i+j+k+4)+1,\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i+j-k+1)+1.

We define
F(x):=\frac{\Gamma_{1}(x)}{\tan(x)}
. Lemma
2.9
applies and since

\displaystyle F\left(0\right)=F^{\prime}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=0,F^{\prime\prime}\left(0\right)=F^{\prime\prime\prime}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=0,F^{(4)}\left(0\right)=F^{(5)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=0,~~~F^{(7)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=\frac{483840}{\pi}\neq 0

we infer

\displaystyle\mathbb{F}_{1jki}=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{8}}\right),

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
. Finally, for large values of
i,j,k
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{F}_{1jki}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\Gamma_{1}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\Gamma_{1}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\omega_{k}.

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
. In conclusion,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}\mathbb{F}_{1jki}=\Bigg\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{8}}\right),&\text{if all }\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty\\
\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}\omega_{k}\right),&\text{if some }\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty,\end{array}

that completes the proof.
∎

and in particular from

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}F(x)\cos((2b+1)x)dx=\sum_{k=0}^{N}\frac{(-1)^{k+b}}{a^{2k+1}}F^{(2k)}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{k=0}^{N}\frac{(-1)^{k+1}}{a^{2k+2}}F^{(2k+1)}\left(0\right)+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2N+2}}\right),

as

\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}
. For large
i
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy

\displaystyle\simeq\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\sin(\omega_{i}y)\cos^{2}(y)dy=-\frac{2}{\omega_{i}(\omega_{i}^{2}-4)}\cos(\omega_{i}x)+\frac{\omega_{i}\cos^{2}(x)}{\omega_{i}^{2}-4}\cos(\omega_{i}x)

\displaystyle+\frac{\sin(2x)}{\omega_{i}^{2}-4}\sin(\omega_{i}x)\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\cos^{2}(x)\cos(\omega_{i}x).

i,j,k
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty
