\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)dx\Bigg).

Observe that all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}
,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i-j+k+1)+1,\quad\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i-j-k-2)+1,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i+j+k+4)+1,\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i+j-k+1)+1.

We define
F(x):=\cos^{2}(x)
and compute

\displaystyle F\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=F^{\prime}\left(0\right)=0,~~~F^{\prime\prime}\left(\frac{\pi}{2}\right)=2\neq 0.

Lemma
2.9
yields

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{3}}\right),

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
, whereas, for large values of
i,j,k
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\int_{\cdot}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\omega_{i}^{-1}.

In conclusion,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{I}}_{jki}=\Bigg\{\begin{array}[]{lr}\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left((\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{-3}\right),&\text{if all }\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{m}\longrightarrow\infty\\
\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}^{-1}\right),&\text{if some }\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty,\end{array}

that completes the proof.
∎

4.4.2.
Fourier constants in
\mathcal{B}

We write

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}=\mathcal{B}_{1}\cup\mathcal{B}_{2}

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}_{1}:=\left\{\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{0jki},\omega_{i}\mathbb{G}_{jki},\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}\right\},\quad\mathcal{B}_{2}:=\left\{\frac{\mathbb{R}_{klji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{S}_{jkli}}{\omega_{i}},\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{klji},\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{ijkl},\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji},\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}\right\}

Proposition 4.5

(Fourier constants in
\mathcal{B}_{1}
)
.

Let
N\in\mathbb{N}
. The following growth and decay estimates hold.

Growth and decay estimates for the Fourier constants in
\mathcal{B}_{1}
as
i,j,k\longrightarrow+\infty

Constant

\exists~\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty

\forall~\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty

\displaystyle~\quad\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{0jki}

\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}\omega_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{N}}\right)

\displaystyle\omega_{i}\mathbb{G}_{jki},\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}

\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}\omega_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{2}}\right)

Proof.

First, observe that

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i-j+k+1)+1,\quad\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i-j-k-2)+1,

\displaystyle\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}

\displaystyle=2(i+j+k+4)+1,\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}=2(i+j-k+1)+1,

are all odd. All results here follow from Lemma
2.6
and Remark
2.7
. For large values of
i,j,k
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}_{0jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)\frac{\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx.

By Remark
2.7
, we infer that in this case

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}_{0jki}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\left(2\left(2\sqrt{2\pi}-2\sqrt{2\pi}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\right)^{N}}\right)\right)=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\right)^{N}}\right),

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
, whereas, for large values of
i,j,k
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{H}_{0jki}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{0}(x)e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{0}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\omega_{k}.

Second, for large values of
i,j,k
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx.

Hence, by Lemma
2.6
, we get that in this case

\displaystyle\mathbb{G}_{jki}=\frac{1}{4}\left(2\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{2}}\right)\right)=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{2}}\right),

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
. On the other hand, for large values of
i,j,k
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{G}_{jki}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|\leq\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{j}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\omega_{k}.

Similarly, for large values of
i,j,k
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin(\omega_{k}x)\cos(\omega_{j}x)\cos(\omega_{i}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

and finally

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}=\frac{1}{4}\left(2\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{2}}\right)\right)=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k})^{2}}\right),

as
i,j,k\longrightarrow\infty
. However, for large values of
i,j,k
such that some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mathbb{G}}_{jki}\right|

\displaystyle:=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{j}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{j}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|\leq\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e^{\prime}_{i}}{\omega_{i}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e^{\prime}_{j}}{\omega_{j}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\lesssim\omega_{k},

that completes the proof.
∎

Proposition 4.6

(Fourier constants in
\mathcal{B}_{2}
)
.

Let
N\in\mathbb{N}
. The following growth and decay estimates hold.

Growth and decay estimates for the Fourier constants in
\mathcal{B}_{2}
as
i,j,k,l\longrightarrow+\infty

Constant

\exists~\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty

\forall~\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty

\displaystyle\quad\frac{\mathbb{R}_{klji}}{\omega_{i}},\frac{\mathbb{S}_{jkli}}{\omega_{i}}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{j}\omega_{i}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{N}}\right)

\displaystyle\omega_{i}\mathbb{H}_{klji},\omega_{i}\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{ijkl}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{i}\omega_{j}\omega_{k}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\omega_{i}}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji},\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}\left(\omega_{j}\omega_{i}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{N}}\right)

Proof.

First, observe that

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}

\displaystyle=2(i-j+k-l),\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}=2(i+j-k-l),

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}

\displaystyle=2(-3+i-j-k-l),\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}=2(3+i+j+k-l),

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}

\displaystyle=2(i-j-k+l),\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}=2(6+i+j+k+l),

\displaystyle\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}

\displaystyle=2(3+i-j+k+l),\quad\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}=2(3+i+j-k+l),

are all even. All results here follow from Lemma
2.6
. For large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{R}_{klji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\cos(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\cos(\omega_{k}x)\cos(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right))}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\left(4\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)^{N}}\right)\right)=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)^{N}}\right).

On the other hand, in the case where some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{m}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\mathbb{R}_{klji}}{\omega_{i}}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\cos(x)\sin(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\cos\sin\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}}{\omega_{k}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}}{\omega_{l}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}}{\omega_{i}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\frac{\omega_{j}}{\omega_{i}},

where we used the
L^{\infty}
bounds as well as the orthogonality from Lemma
2.4
. Furthermore, for large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{S}_{jkli}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{\sin^{3}(x)}{\cos(x)}dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\cos(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)\sin(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle=4\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)^{N}}\right)=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)^{N}}\right).

On the other hand, in the case where some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\mathbb{S}_{jkli}}{\omega_{i}}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\cos(x)\sin(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\cos\sin\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}}{\omega_{i}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{l}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\frac{\omega_{j}}{\omega_{i}},

where we used the
L^{\infty}
bounds and the orthogonality from Lemma
2.4
. Similarly, for large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{H}_{klji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)\sin(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{c}{16\pi}\Big(+(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})+(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})-(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})

\displaystyle-(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})+(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})+(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})

\displaystyle-(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})-(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})\Big)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{3}}\right).

On the other hand, in the case where some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{H}_{klji}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(x)e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{i}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{l}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\omega_{j}\omega_{k}.

Furthermore, for large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{ijkl}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\tan^{2}(x)dx\simeq\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)\cos(\omega_{i}x)\cos(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\frac{2\pi}{\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}}}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\cos\left(\left(\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l}\right)x\right)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

and the rest of the proof coincides with the one above. On the other hand, if some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mathbb{H}}_{ijkl}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{i}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{i}^{\prime}}{\omega_{i}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}}{\omega_{l}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\omega_{j}\omega_{k}.

In addition, for large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\cos(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\cos(\omega_{k}x)\cos(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{8\omega_{i}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\omega_{i}}\left(4\left(\frac{\pi}{2}-\frac{\pi}{2}\right)+\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{N}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\sum_{\pm}\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{(\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l})^{N}}\right).

On the other hand, in the case where some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mathbb{R}}_{klji}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{k}}\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}(x)}{\omega_{l}}\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\int_{\cdot}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{k}^{\prime}}{\omega_{k}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\frac{e_{l}^{\prime}}{\omega_{l}}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\frac{\omega_{j}}{\omega_{i}}.

Similarly, for large values of
i,j,k,l
and in the case where all
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longrightarrow\infty
,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{\omega_{i}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\cos(\omega_{i}x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)\sin(\omega_{k}x)\sin(\omega_{l}x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{8\omega_{i}}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}+\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}-\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}+\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx-\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)\frac{\sin((\omega_{i}-\omega_{j}+\omega_{k}-\omega_{l})x)}{\tan(x)}dx\Bigg)

and the rest of the proof coincides with the one above. On the other hand, if some
\omega_{i}\pm\omega_{j}\pm\omega_{k}\pm\omega_{l}\longarrownot\longrightarrow\infty
, Holder’s inequality implies

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mathbb{S}}_{jkli}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{j}(x)e_{k}(x)e_{l}(x)\left(\int_{x}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|e_{j}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|\int_{\cdot}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}e_{i}(y)\sin(y)\cos(y)dy\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{k}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\left\|e_{l}\tan\right\|_{L^{2}\left[0,\frac{\pi}{2}\right]}\leq\frac{\omega_{j}}{\omega_{i}},

that completes the proof.
∎

Appendix A
Auxiliary integral estimates

In this section, we prove the estimate (
2.11
). To do so, we will use the notation

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}\left(\text{condition}\right)=\begin{cases}1,\quad\text{if the condition if satisfied}\\
0,\quad\text{otherwise}\end{cases}

Lemma A.1
.

For all
i=0,1,2,\dots
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(\omega_{i}x)\tan^{2}(x)dx=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin^{2}(\omega_{i}x)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx=\frac{\pi}{2}\left(\omega_{i}-\frac{1}{2}\right).

Proof.

Both results follow from Lemma
2.5
. For the first result, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(\omega_{i}x)\tan^{2}(x)dx=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\frac{\cos(\omega_{i}x)}{\cos(x)}\right)^{2}\sin^{2}(x)dx=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(1+2\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}(-1)^{\mu}\cos(2\mu x)\right)^{2}\sin^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(1+4\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}(-1)^{\mu}\cos(2\mu x)+4\sum_{\mu,\nu=1}^{i+1}(-1)^{\mu+\nu}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\right)\sin^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\sin^{2}(x)dx+4\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}(-1)^{\mu}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\sin^{2}(x)dx+4\sum_{\mu,\nu=1}^{i+1}(-1)^{\mu+\nu}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\sin^{2}(x)dx.

Now, we use various trigonometric identities
to compute

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\sin^{2}(x)dx=\frac{\pi}{4},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\sin^{2}(x)dx=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2(\mu-1)x)dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2(\mu+1)x)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=-\frac{\pi}{8}\mathbbm{1}\left(\mu=1\right),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\sin^{2}(x)dx=-\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1-\mu-\nu)x\right)dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(\mu-\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1+\mu-\nu)x\right)dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1-\mu+\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(\mu+\nu)x\right)dx-\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1+\mu+\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle=-\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1-\mu-\nu=0\right)+\frac{\pi}{8}\mathbbm{1}\left(\mu-\nu=0\right)-\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1+\mu-\nu=0\right)-\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1-\mu+\nu=0\right).

Hence,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(\omega_{i}x)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1-\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1+\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1-\mu+\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot 0+\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot(1+i)+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot i+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot i=\frac{\pi}{2}\left(\omega_{i}-\frac{1}{2}\right).

Similarly, for the second result, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin^{2}(\omega_{i}x)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\frac{\sin(\omega_{i}x)}{\sin(x)}\right)^{2}\cos^{2}(x)dx=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(1+2\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}\cos(2\mu x)\right)^{2}\cos^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(1+4\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}\cos(2\mu x)+4\sum_{\mu,\nu=1}^{i+1}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\right)\cos^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)dx+4\sum_{\mu=1}^{i+1}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos^{2}(x)dx+4\sum_{\mu,\nu=1}^{i+1}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\cos^{2}(x)dx.

Now, we use various trigonometric identities
to compute

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos^{2}(x)dx=\frac{\pi}{4},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos^{2}(x)dx=\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2(\mu-1)x)dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2(\mu+1)x)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=\frac{\pi}{8}\mathbbm{1}\left(\mu=1\right),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos(2\mu x)\cos(2\nu x)\cos^{2}(x)dx=\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1-\mu-\nu)x\right)dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(\mu-\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1+\mu-\nu)x\right)dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1-\mu+\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(\mu+\nu)x\right)dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\cos\left(2(1+\mu+\nu)x\right)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1-\mu-\nu=0\right)+\frac{\pi}{8}\mathbbm{1}\left(\mu-\nu=0\right)+\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1+\mu-\nu=0\right)+\frac{\pi}{16}\mathbbm{1}\left(1-\mu+\nu=0\right).

Hence,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{\sin^{2}(\omega_{i}x)}{\tan^{2}(x)}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1-\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1+\mu-\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1+\frac{\pi}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu,\nu=1\\
1-\mu+\nu=0\end{subarray}}^{i+1}1

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{4}+\frac{\pi}{2}+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot 0+\frac{\pi}{2}\cdot(1+i)+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot i+\frac{\pi}{4}\cdot i

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{2}\left(\omega_{i}-\frac{1}{2}\right),

that completes the proof.
∎

\frac{8116251}{143360\pi^{2}}\sqrt{3},\quad j=1\\[10.0pt]
-\frac{3585921}{71680\pi^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}},\quad j=2\\[10.0pt]
-\frac{89919801}{788480\pi^{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{10}},\quad j=3\\[10.0pt]

\displaystyle\cdots\frac{-1590653760j^{3}-30109600j^{4}+80991360j^{5}+2020992j^{6}-1827840j^{7}}{(-5+2j)(-3+2j)(-1+2j)(1+2j)(5+2j)(7+2j)}\cdots

\displaystyle\cdots\frac{-83200j^{8}+15360j^{9}+1024j^{10}}{(9+2j)(11+2j)(13+2j)(15+2j)(17+2j)(19+2j)},\quad j\geq 0.

Proof.

All these closed formulas are based on Lemma
2.2
and follow similarly. Therefore, we illustrate the proof only for the first constant, namely for
\mathbb{C}_{130j}
. For all
j=0,1,2,\dots
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{C}_{130j}

\displaystyle:=\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{0}(x)e_{j}(x)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{2}-1}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{0}(x)\left(\omega_{j}\frac{\sin(\omega_{j}x)}{\tan(x)}-\cos(\omega_{j}x)\right)\tan^{2}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{j}}{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{2}-1}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}w_{1}(x)\sin(\omega_{j}x)dx-\frac{1}{\sqrt{\omega_{j}^{2}-1}}\int_{0}^{\frac{\pi}{2}}w_{2}(x)\cos(\omega_{j}x)dx,

(B.1)

where

\displaystyle w_{1}(x)

\displaystyle:=\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{0}(x)\tan(x)=\frac{3}{4\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}}\cos^{2}(x)q(x),

\displaystyle w_{2}(x)

\displaystyle:=\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\left(\Gamma_{1}(x)-\Gamma_{3}(x)\right)e_{0}(x)\tan^{2}(x)=\frac{3}{8\sqrt{2}\pi^{2}}\sin(2x)q(x)

and

\displaystyle q(x):=384~x\cos^{3}(x)-254\sin(x)-8\sin(3x)-16\sin(5x)-5\sin(7x)+\sin(9x).

We use trigonometric identities to write

\displaystyle w_{1}(x)

\displaystyle=x\left(\frac{90\sqrt{2}}{\pi^{2}}\cos(x)+\frac{45\sqrt{2}}{\pi^{2}}\cos(3x)+\frac{9\sqrt{2}}{\pi^{2}}\cos(5x)\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{393}{8\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(x)-\frac{429}{8\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(3x)-\frac{135}{16\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(5x)-\frac{75}{16\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(7x)

\displaystyle-\frac{9}{16\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(9x)+\frac{3}{16\pi^{2}\sqrt{2}}\sin(11x).

For
j\geq 5
, we compute each integral separately and find a closed formula for the first integral in (
B.1
). Similarly, for the second. All the other values
\mathbb{C}_{130j}
,
j\in\{0,1,2,3,4\}
are computed with Mathematica.
∎
